# Unicode-mode single-pattern tests for regexped.
#
# Every block runs in Unicode mode (`make custom-tests-unicode`, with -unicode
# -unicode-oracle). The expectation rows are Go's answers over each pattern with
# U+FFFD taken out of its classes and `.`, so an invalid byte matches nothing, as
# in Unicode mode — REGENERATED, never hand-edited:
#
#     go run ./make_adjusted -unicode-regen custom-tests-unicode.txt
#
# Edit the blocks, strings and patterns; rerun the command. Columns: col0 the
# anchored match (groups export: the first match, if it starts at 0) or the
# full-consumption match, col1 the first match, col4 every match, and for a
# pattern tested through the groups export col5 (first match with captures)
# and col6 (every match with captures).
# ============================================================
# \p{..} / \P{..} classes, by category and by script, over valid and invalid input.
# ============================================================
